# IMPERIA CW — Cold War: South Korean presentation.
COLDWAR_KOR_campaign = {
	hidden_trigger = { tag = KOR }
	hidden_trigger = { original_tag = KOR }
	hidden_trigger = { IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes }
}
COLDWAR_KOR_has_vietnam_commitment = {
	custom_trigger_tooltip = {
		tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_vietnam_commitment_active_tt
		not_tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_vietnam_commitment_active_tt_NOT
		has_country_flag = COLDWAR_KOR_vietnam_commitment_active
	}
}
COLDWAR_KOR_shared_channel_active = {
	country_exists = KOR
	country_exists = NKO
	KOR = {
		hidden_trigger = { IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes }
		NOT = { has_war_with = NKO }
	}
	OR = {
		AND = {
			KOR = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_working_contacts_signed_tt
					not_tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_working_contacts_signed_tt_NOT
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_KOR_working_contacts_signed
				}
			}
			NKO = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_working_contacts_signed_tt
					not_tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_working_contacts_signed_tt_NOT
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_KOR_working_contacts_signed
				}
			}
		}
		AND = {
			KOR = { has_idea = COLDWAR_KOR_confederal_member }
			NKO = { has_idea = COLDWAR_KOR_confederal_member }
		}
		AND = {
			KOR = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_legacy_working_contacts_tt
					not_tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_legacy_working_contacts_tt_NOT
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_KOR_legacy_working_contacts
				}
			}
			NKO = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_legacy_working_contacts_tt
					not_tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_legacy_working_contacts_tt_NOT
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_KOR_legacy_working_contacts
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_KOR_initialized }

		not_tooltip = COLDWAR_KOR_require_combined_exercises_paid_tt_NOT
		has_country_flag = COLDWAR_KOR_combined_exercises_paid
	}
	hidden_trigger = {
		check_variable = { COLDWAR_KOR_slot_national_usfk_exercise_staff = 300 }
	}
	has_idea = COLDWAR_KOR_usfk_member
	USA = { has_idea = COLDWAR_KOR_usfk_member }
}
